================================================================================
SHC STOCK — ARCHIVED STATIC / HARDCODED DATA
================================================================================
This file is a permanent archive of every static/hardcoded data list that
used to live directly in the Flutter source code, before each module was
made fully dynamic (backend API + PostgreSQL via Prisma, Dio-based
ApiClient). The source files have since had these blocks DELETED (they were
previously kept as commented-out code for reference; that reference now
lives here instead).

Where the exact same data was also seeded into the database via a backend
script, that script is noted — run it again any time to restore this
reference data into a fresh/empty DB.

Archived: 2026-08-09
================================================================================


--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. PRODUCTS — Category/Sub-category taxonomy
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Source: lib/app/modules/products/controllers/products_controller.dart
Was: static final List<ProductCategory> allCategories
Replaced by: ProductsController.realCategories (reads live from
             CategoriesController / GET /api/categories)
Backend equivalent: backend/prisma/seed.js (same 7 categories + sub-categories,
                     seeded into the categories/sub_categories tables)

  1. Ceramic Fiber Products
     - Ceramic Fiber Blanket
     - Ceramic Fiber Bulk (Loose Fiber)
     - Ceramic Fiber Module
     - Ceramic Fiber Board
     - Ceramic Fiber Paper
     - Heatshield Blanket

  2. Ceramic Fiber Textile
     - Ceramic Fiber Rope
     - Ceramic Fiber Cloth
     - Ceramic Fiber Tape

  3. Insulation Bricks
     - HF/CF Insulation Bricks
     - HFK Insulation Bricks
     - Porosint Bricks

  4. Refractories
     - Fire Bricks
     - Sillimanite & Mullite Bricks
     - Burner Block
     - Hollow Bricks

  5. Mortars & Castables
     - Refractory Mortar (ORTEX)
     - Air Setting Mortar (ORTEX-HT)
     - Castable Refractory

  6. Fire & Welding Protection
     - Fire Blanket
     - Welding Blanket

  7. Accessories & Services
     - Stud & Washer Anchor
     - Ceramic Fiber Lining Anchor
     - Brick Anchor
     - Refractory Anchor
     - Hot Insulation Service

po_15 | PO-1300 | Ashoka Metals (AM)           | 2026-06-05 | items:1 | ₹33000  | Pending   | Suresh Kumar (2026-06-05 14:20)

po_16..po_28 — generated programmatically (not hand-authored):
  for i in 16..28:
    poNumber      = "PO-" + (1300 - (i-15)*120)
    supplier      = cycle through [Ashoka Metals(AM), Gujarat Polymers(GP),
                    ThermoTech Industries(TT), Precision Valves Co.(PV),
                    National Insulation Co.(NI)] by (i-16) % 5
    date          = 2026-06-(4-(i-16))   [dates go backward, can go negative-day
                    which Dart normalizes into earlier months]
    itemCount     = i % 3 + 1
    amount        = i * 3450.0
    status        = PurchaseStatus.values[i % 4]  (received/partial/pending/cancelled cycle)
    modifiedBy    = "Admin", modifiedAt = null

so_16..so_156 — generated programmatically (141 more orders, not hand-authored):
  clients cycle (10, by (i-1) % 10): Arvind Enterprises(AE), Shree Ram Traders(SRT),
    Jai Mata Di Suppliers(JMD), Gupta Hardware(GH), National Insulation Co.(NIC),
    Buildwell Supply(BS), Om Sai Traders(OST), Shakti Enterprises(SE),
    Lakshmi Industries(LI), Rajesh & Sons(RS)
  for i in 16..156:
    soNumber   = "SO-2024-00" + (156-i+1), left-padded to 11 chars
    client     = clients[(i-1) % 10]
    date       = 2024-05-18 minus (i-15) days
    itemCount  = (i % 15) + 3
    amount     = ((i * 4350) % 98000 + 12000)
    status     = SalesStatus.values[(i-1) % 5]      (confirmed/shipped/delivered/cancelled/processing cycle)
    payment    = PaymentStatus.values[(i-1) % 4]    (paid/partial/refunded/pending cycle)

11. CLIENTS — 1037 rows (reference snapshot — NOT the source of truth)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Source: lib/app/modules/clients/data/clients_seed_data.dart (already deleted)
Replaced by: ClientsController → GET /api/clients

The `clients` table in the live PostgreSQL database is the actual source of
truth — this JSON block is a point-in-time reference copy only, kept here for
history. No script reads this file; there is deliberately no code anywhere
that depends on static_data.txt. For a real backup/restore of the `clients`
table, use a proper Postgres dump (e.g. `pg_dump`/`pg_restore`), not this
